window when multiple are open\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e👥 \u003cstrong\u003eMulti-User\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eShare bot control with your team via comma-separated Chat IDs\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e💬 \u003cstrong\u003eThread Management\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eList, switch, and manage chat threads (agent conversations)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e⚡ \u003cstrong\u003eAuto-Accept\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eAutomatically click Run, Accept, Allow, Continue buttons via a DOM MutationObserver\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e🚀 \u003cstrong\u003eTurbo Mode\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eMulti-agent orchestration: Claude plans → Gemini codes → Claude reviews → Gemini fixes\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e🎯 \u003cstrong\u003eGoal Mode\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eAutonomous long-running tasks — agent works until the goal is fully achieved\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e📋 \u003cstrong\u003ePlan Mode\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eGenerate implementation plans before coding\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e🔔 \u003cstrong\u003eProactive Notifications\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eTaskWatcher detects unsolicited agent messages (timers, sub-agents) and forwards to Telegram\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e🤔 \u003cstrong\u003eMessage Reactions\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eShows 🤔 while processing, clears when done\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e🔄 \u003cstrong\u003eAuto-Update\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eCheck for updates and self-update with one command\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e🌐 \u003cstrong\u003eMulti-Language\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e7 languages supported: English, Chinese, Korean, Turkish, German, Spanish, French\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e⌨️ \u003cstrong\u003eTyping Indicator\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eShows \"typing...\" in Telegram while the agent is working\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e🖥️ \u003cstrong\u003eCross-Platform\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eWorks on Linux, macOS (Intel \u0026amp; Apple Silicon), and Windows\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e🔀 \u003cstrong\u003eDual App Support\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eSeamlessly switch between Antigravity IDE and Standalone Agent App\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"cp .env.example .env\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003ecp .env.example .env\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eEdit \u003ccode\u003e.env\u003c/code\u003e with your values:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-dotenv notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# Telegram\nBOT_TOKEN=your_telegram_bot_token\nALLOWED_CHAT_ID=your_chat_id,another_chat_id_optional\n\n# CDP Debugging Ports (must match the --remote-debugging-port used when launching)\nAGENT_CDP_PORT=9333    # Port for the Standalone Antigravity App\nIDE_CDP_PORT=9334      # Port for the Antigravity IDE\n\n# Default AI model to select on new chat\nDEFAULT_MODEL=Gemini 3.1 Pro (High)\n\n# Language: en | zh | ko | tr | de | es | fr\nLANGUAGE=en\n\n# Preferred app target: 'agent' (Standalone) or 'ide' (IDE)\nANTIGRAVITY_PREFERRED_APP=ide\n\n# Enable auto-accept by default\nAUTOACCEPT_DEFAULT=true\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Telegram\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003eBOT_TOKEN\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003eyour_telegram_bot_token\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003eALLOWED_CHAT_ID\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003eyour_chat_id,another_chat_id_optional\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e CDP Debugging Ports (must match the --remote-debugging-port used when launching)\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003eAGENT_CDP_PORT\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e9333\u003c/span\u003e    \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Port for the Standalone Antigravity App\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003eIDE_CDP_PORT\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e9334\u003c/span\u003e      \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Port for the Antigravity IDE\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Default AI model to select on new chat\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003eDEFAULT_MODEL\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003eGemini 3.1 Pro (High)\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Language: en | zh | ko | tr | de | es | fr\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003eLANGUAGE\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003een\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Preferred app target: 'agent' (Standalone) or 'ide' (IDE)\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003eANTIGRAVITY_PREFERRED_APP\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003eide\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Enable auto-accept by default\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003eAUTOACCEPT_DEFAULT\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003etrue\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e💡 Send \u003ccode\u003e/start\u003c/code\u003e to your bot to get your Chat ID.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e3. Launch the App with CDP\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-3-launch-the-app-with-cdp\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 3. Launch the App with CDP\" href=\"#3-launch-the-app-with-cdp\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe bot communicates with Antigravity via Chrome DevTools Protocol (CDP). You must launch the app with a debugging port.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eIf running both apps side-by-side, use different ports:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# --- Standalone Antigravity App ---\n# Linux\nantigravity --remote-debugging-port=9333\n\n# macOS\nopen -a Antigravity --args --remote-debugging-port=9333\n\n# Windows\nAntigravity.exe --remote-debugging-port=9333\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e --- Standalone Antigravity App ---\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Linux\u003c/span\u003e\nantigravity --remote-debugging-port=9333\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e macOS\u003c/span\u003e\nopen -a Antigravity --args --remote-debugging-port=9333\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Windows\u003c/span\u003e\nAntigravity.exe --remote-debugging-port=9333\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# --- Antigravity IDE ---\n# Linux\nantigravity-ide --remote-debugging-port=9334\n\n# macOS\nopen -a \u0026quot;Antigravity IDE\u0026quot; --args --remote-debugging-port=9334\n\n# Windows\n\u0026quot;Antigravity IDE.exe\u0026quot; --remote-debugging-port=9334\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e --- Antigravity IDE ---\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Linux\u003c/span\u003e\nantigravity-ide --remote-debugging-port=9334\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e macOS\u003c/span\u003e\nopen -a \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eAntigravity IDE\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e --args --remote-debugging-port=9334\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Windows\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eAntigravity IDE.exe\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e --remote-debugging-port=9334\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cg-emoji class=\"g-emoji\" alias=\"warning\"\u003e⚠️\u003c/g-emoji\u003e The port numbers must match \u003ccode\u003eAGENT_CDP_PORT\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003eIDE_CDP_PORT\u003c/code\u003e in your \u003ccode\u003e.env\u003c/code\u003e file.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e4.                                                     │\n│  Phase 1: PLANNING        Claude Opus → Creates implementation plan │\n│  Phase 2: CODING          Gemini Pro  → Writes the code class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003e┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                        TURBO MODE PIPELINE                         │\n│                                                                     │\n│  Phase 1: PLANNING        Claude Opus → Creates implementation plan │\n│  Phase 2: CODING          Gemini Pro  → Writes the code             │\n│  Phase 3: REVIEW          Claude Opus → Security \u0026amp; code review      │\n│  Phase 4: FIX (if needed) Gemini Pro  → Fixes issues found          │\n│  Phase 5: SUMMARY         Gemini Pro  → Executive summary for user  │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eHow to use:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eEnable Turbo Mode: \u003ccode\u003e/turbo\u003c/code\u003e → Select \"Enable\"\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eSend your request as normal text\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThe bot will automatically switch models and run all phases\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eYou'll receive real-time phase updates and a final summary\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e💡 Turbo Mode requires access to both Claude and Gemini models in works\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eAgent works autonomously in a single session until done\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eBot orchestrates multi-model pipeline externally\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eModels used\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eWhichever model is currently selected\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eClaude (plan/review) + Gemini (code/fix) — automatic switching\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eKey advantage\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eSimple, reliable, native IDE feature\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eMulti-model collaboration: different models cross-check each other\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eToken usage\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eSingle context window (efficient)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eMultiple round-trips (more tokens)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eProgress\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e🤔 reaction → final result\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eReal-time pinned message with phase updates\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eBest for\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eLong tasks with a single model\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eComplex tasks benefiting from multi-model review\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eArchitecture\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eIDE-native (\u003ccode\u003e/goal\u003c/code\u003e slash command)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eExternal orchestration via CDP + \u003ccode\u003eturbo_orchestrator.js\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWhen to use which:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSimple long task\u003c/strong\u003e (e.g., \"refactor this module\") → \u003ccode\u003e/goal\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eComplex task needing cross-model review\u003c/strong\u003e (e.g., \"build this feature, review security, fix issues\") → \u003ccode\u003e/turbo\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ePlanning\u003c/strong\u003e → \u003ccode\u003e/plan\u003c/code\u003e class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003e┌──────────┐     Telegram API     ┌──────────────┐     CDP (WebSocket)     ┌─────────────────┐\n│ Telegram │ ◄──────────────────► │ Antigravity  │ ◄────────────────────► │ Antigravity IDE  │\n│   App    │     Bot Commands     │     Bot      │    DOM Interaction     │       or         │\n└──────────┘                      └──────────────┘                        │ Standalone Agent │\n                                                                          └─────────────────┘\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eYou send a message via Telegram\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThe bot injects your text into the AI agent's chat input via CDP\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThe bot monitors the agent for completion (typing indicator shown in Telegram)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eOnce done, the response is extracted and sent back to Telegram\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAuto-Accept\u003c/strong\u003e: When enabled, a MutationObserver watches for action buttons (Run, Accept, Allow, Continue) and clicks them automatically\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eDual App 2.0\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eIf Antigravity IDE auto-updates, DOM selectors may break until the bot is also updated.\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eTurbo Mode Model Access\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eTurbo Mode requires both Claude and Gemini models to be available. If one model is unavailable, the pipeline will fail.\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e💡 As a developer, I prefer to focus on IDE support. <div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🤖 Antigravity Telegram Suite</h1><a id="user-content--antigravity-telegram-suite" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🤖 Antigravity Telegram Suite" href="#-antigravity-telegram-suite"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Works with both <a href="https://antigravity.google/" rel="nofollow">Antigravity Standalone App</a>* and <a href="https://antigravity.google/" rel="nofollow">Antigravity IDE</a>.</strong></p>
<p dir="auto">🌍 Languages: <a href="/emreturkmencom/antigravity-telegram-suite/blob/main/README.md">English</a> | <a href="/emreturkmencom/antigravity-telegram-suite/blob/main/README.zh.md">中文</a> | <a href="/emreturkmencom/antigravity-telegram-suite/blob/main/README.ko.md">한국어</a> | <a href="/emreturkmencom/antigravity-telegram-suite/blob/main/README.tr.md">Türkçe</a> | <a href="/emreturkmencom/antigravity-telegram-suite/blob/main/README.de.md">Deutsch</a> | <a href="/emreturkmencom/antigravity-telegram-suite/blob/main/README.es.md">Español</a> | <a href="/emreturkmencom/antigravity-telegram-suite/blob/main/README.fr.md">Français</a></p>
<p dir="auto">Control your Antigravity AI agent remotely via Telegram.
Send messages, switch AI models, manage workspaces, take screenshots, and run multi-agent workflows — all from your phone.</p>
<p dir="auto"><a href="/emreturkmencom/antigravity-telegram-suite/blob/main/LICENSE"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/08cef40a9105b6526ca22088bc514fbfdbc9aac1ddbf8d4e6c750e3a88a44dca/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f62616467652f4c6963656e73652d4d49542d626c75652e737667" alt="License: MIT" data-canonical-src="https://img.shields.io/badge/License-MIT-blue.svg" style="max-width: 100%;"></a>
<a href="https://nodejs.org" rel="nofollow"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/1510c55912551c03658e02ce3409d6d5e3939415f4d06d8466a4fed79edf8dc4/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f62616467652f4e6f64652e6a732d25334525334431382d677265656e2e737667" alt="Node.js" data-canonical-src="https://img.shields.io/badge/Node.js-%3E%3D18-green.svg" style="max-width: 100%;"></a>
<a href="/emreturkmencom/antigravity-telegram-suite/blob/main"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/b740e4b9688c5eae2a514fac816d31e39cec3bc94417294f22ce773a80a297a3/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f62616467652f506c6174666f726d2d4c696e75782532302537432532306d61634f5325323025374325323057696e646f77732d6c69676874677265792e737667" alt="Platform" data-canonical-src="https://img.shields.io/badge/Platform-Linux%20%7C%20macOS%20%7C%20Windows-lightgrey.svg" style="max-width: 100%;"></a>
<a href="/emreturkmencom/antigravity-telegram-suite/blob/main"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/cc020b91cd9231e992464653522a0cda5aa6f76d9e8b1aa23235749d0dc77038/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f62616467652f56657273696f6e2d332e342e302d6f72616e67652e737667" alt="Version" data-canonical-src="https://img.shields.io/badge/Version-3.4.0-orange.svg" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<p dir="auto">* <em>Some features may have limitations on the Standalone App. See <a href="#-known-issues">Known Issues</a>.</em></p>
</div>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">✨ Features</h2><a id="user-content--features" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: ✨ Features" href="#-features"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Feature</th>
<th>Description</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>💬 <strong>Headless Chat</strong></td>
<td>Send messages directly to the AI agent via Telegram</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>📎 <strong>File &amp; Image Upload</strong></td>
<td>Forward files/images to the agent with captions</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>📸 <strong>IDE Screenshots</strong></td>
<td>Capture and receive screenshots remotely</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>🤖 <strong>Model Switching</strong></td>
<td>Change AI models (Gemini, Claude, GPT) with inline buttons</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>📂 <strong>File Explorer</strong></td>
<td>Browse, navigate, and download project files</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>🔄 <strong>Workspace Management</strong></td>
<td>Switch between projects without touching the keyboard</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>🪟 <strong>Multi-Window Support</strong></td>
<td>Route commands to a specific IDE window when multiple are open</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>👥 <strong>Multi-User</strong></td>
<td>Share bot control with your team via comma-separated Chat IDs</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>💬 <strong>Thread Management</strong></td>
<td>List, switch, and manage chat threads (agent conversations)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>⚡ <strong>Auto-Accept</strong></td>
<td>Automatically click Run, Accept, Allow, Continue buttons via a DOM MutationObserver</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>🚀 <strong>Turbo Mode</strong></td>
<td>Multi-agent orchestration: Claude plans → Gemini codes → Claude reviews → Gemini fixes</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>🎯 <strong>Goal Mode</strong></td>
<td>Autonomous long-running tasks — agent works until the goal is fully achieved</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>📋 <strong>Plan Mode</strong></td>
<td>Generate implementation plans before coding</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>🔔 <strong>Proactive Notifications</strong></td>
<td>TaskWatcher detects unsolicited agent messages (timers, sub-agents) and forwards to Telegram</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>🤔 <strong>Message Reactions</strong></td>
<td>Shows 🤔 while processing, clears when done</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>🔄 <strong>Auto-Update</strong></td>
<td>Check for updates and self-update with one command</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>🌐 <strong>Multi-Language</strong></td>
<td>7 languages supported: English, Chinese, Korean, Turkish, German, Spanish, French</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>⌨️ <strong>Typing Indicator</strong></td>
<td>Shows "typing..." in Telegram while the agent is working</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>🖥️ <strong>Cross-Platform</strong></td>
<td>Works on Linux, macOS (Intel &amp; Apple Silicon), and Windows</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>🔀 <strong>Dual App Support</strong></td>
<td>Seamlessly switch between Antigravity IDE and Standalone Agent App</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🚀 Quick Start</h2><a id="user-content--quick-start" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🚀 Quick Start" href="#-quick-start"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Prerequisites</h3><a id="user-content-prerequisites" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Prerequisites" href="#prerequisites"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://nodejs.org/" rel="nofollow">Node.js</a> &gt;= 18</li>
<li><a href="https://antigravity.google/" rel="nofollow">Antigravity IDE</a> and/or <a href="https://antigravity.google/" rel="nofollow">Antigravity Standalone App</a> installed</li>
<li>A Telegram bot token (get one from <a href="https://t.me/BotFather" rel="nofollow">@BotFather</a>)</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">1. Clone &amp; Install</h3><a id="user-content-1-clone--install" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 1. Clone &amp; Install" href="#1-clone--install"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="git clone https://github.com/emreturkmencom/antigravity-telegram-suite.git
cd antigravity-telegram-suite
npm install"><pre>git clone https://github.com/emreturkmencom/antigravity-telegram-suite.git
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> antigravity-telegram-suite
npm install</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">2. Configure</h3><a id="user-content-2-configure" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 2. Configure" href="#2-configure"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="cp .env.example .env"><pre>cp .env.example .env</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Edit <code>.env</code> with your values:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-dotenv notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Telegram
BOT_TOKEN=your_telegram_bot_token
ALLOWED_CHAT_ID=your_chat_id,another_chat_id_optional

# CDP Debugging Ports (must match the --remote-debugging-port used when launching)
AGENT_CDP_PORT=9333    # Port for the Standalone Antigravity App
IDE_CDP_PORT=9334      # Port for the Antigravity IDE

# Default AI model to select on new chat
DEFAULT_MODEL=Gemini 3.1 Pro (High)

# Language: en | zh | ko | tr | de | es | fr
LANGUAGE=en

# Preferred app target: 'agent' (Standalone) or 'ide' (IDE)
ANTIGRAVITY_PREFERRED_APP=ide

# Enable auto-accept by default
AUTOACCEPT_DEFAULT=true"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Telegram</span>
<span class="pl-v">BOT_TOKEN</span><span class="pl-k">=</span><span class="pl-s">your_telegram_bot_token</span>
<span class="pl-v">ALLOWED_CHAT_ID</span><span class="pl-k">=</span><span class="pl-s">your_chat_id,another_chat_id_optional</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> CDP Debugging Ports (must match the --remote-debugging-port used when launching)</span>
<span class="pl-v">AGENT_CDP_PORT</span><span class="pl-k">=</span><span class="pl-s">9333</span>    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Port for the Standalone Antigravity App</span>
<span class="pl-v">IDE_CDP_PORT</span><span class="pl-k">=</span><span class="pl-s">9334</span>      <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Port for the Antigravity IDE</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Default AI model to select on new chat</span>
<span class="pl-v">DEFAULT_MODEL</span><span class="pl-k">=</span><span class="pl-s">Gemini 3.1 Pro (High)</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Language: en | zh | ko | tr | de | es | fr</span>
<span class="pl-v">LANGUAGE</span><span class="pl-k">=</span><span class="pl-s">en</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Preferred app target: 'agent' (Standalone) or 'ide' (IDE)</span>
<span class="pl-v">ANTIGRAVITY_PREFERRED_APP</span><span class="pl-k">=</span><span class="pl-s">ide</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Enable auto-accept by default</span>
<span class="pl-v">AUTOACCEPT_DEFAULT</span><span class="pl-k">=</span><span class="pl-s">true</span></pre></div>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">💡 Send <code>/start</code> to your bot to get your Chat ID.</p>
</blockquote>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">3. Launch the App with CDP</h3><a id="user-content-3-launch-the-app-with-cdp" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 3. Launch the App with CDP" href="#3-launch-the-app-with-cdp"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The bot communicates with Antigravity via Chrome DevTools Protocol (CDP). You must launch the app with a debugging port.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>If running both apps side-by-side, use different ports:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# --- Standalone Antigravity App ---
# Linux
antigravity --remote-debugging-port=9333

# macOS
open -a Antigravity --args --remote-debugging-port=9333

# Windows
Antigravity.exe --remote-debugging-port=9333"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> --- Standalone Antigravity App ---</span>
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Linux</span>
antigravity --remote-debugging-port=9333

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> macOS</span>
open -a Antigravity --args --remote-debugging-port=9333

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Windows</span>
Antigravity.exe --remote-debugging-port=9333</pre></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# --- Antigravity IDE ---
# Linux
antigravity-ide --remote-debugging-port=9334

# macOS
open -a &quot;Antigravity IDE&quot; --args --remote-debugging-port=9334

# Windows
&quot;Antigravity IDE.exe&quot; --remote-debugging-port=9334"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> --- Antigravity IDE ---</span>
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Linux</span>
antigravity-ide --remote-debugging-port=9334

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> macOS</span>
open -a <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Antigravity IDE<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> --args --remote-debugging-port=9334

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Windows</span>
<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Antigravity IDE.exe<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> --remote-debugging-port=9334</pre></div>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><g-emoji class="g-emoji" alias="warning">⚠️</g-emoji> The port numbers must match <code>AGENT_CDP_PORT</code> and <code>IDE_CDP_PORT</code> in your <code>.env</code> file.</p>
</blockquote>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">4. Start the Bot</h3><a id="user-content-4-start-the-bot" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 4. Start the Bot" href="#4-start-the-bot"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<p dir="auto">For 24/7 operation with PM2:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="npm install -g pm2
pm2 start src/index.js --name antigravity-bot
pm2 save
pm2 startup"><pre>npm install -g pm2
pm2 start src/index.js --name antigravity-bot
pm2 save
pm2 startup</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Automated Setup (Optional)</h3><a id="user-content-automated-setup-optional" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Automated Setup (Optional)" href="#automated-setup-optional"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<tr>

<td>Start <strong>Goal Mode</strong> — agent works autonomously until done</td>

<td>Generate an <strong>implementation plan</strong> before coding</td>
</tr>

</tr>
<tr>

<td>List and switch between chat threads</td>
</tr>
<tr>

<td>Check AI credits and model usage limits</td>
</tr>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🚀 Turbo Mode (Multi-Agent Orchestration)</h2><a id="user-content--turbo-mode-multi-agent-orchestration" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🚀 Turbo Mode (Multi-Agent Orchestration)" href="#-turbo-mode-multi-agent-orchestration"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Turbo Mode runs an <strong>Agents Council</strong> workflow that coordinates multiple AI models automatically:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                        TURBO MODE PIPELINE                         │
│                                                                     │
│  Phase 1: PLANNING        Claude Opus → Creates implementation plan │
│  Phase 2: CODING          Gemini Pro  → Writes the code             │
│  Phase 3: REVIEW          Claude Opus → Security &amp; code review      │
│  Phase 4: FIX (if needed) Gemini Pro  → Fixes issues found          │
│  Phase 5: SUMMARY         Gemini Pro  → Executive summary for user  │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘"><pre class="notranslate"><code>┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                        TURBO MODE PIPELINE                         │
│                                                                     │
│  Phase 1: PLANNING        Claude Opus → Creates implementation plan │
│  Phase 2: CODING          Gemini Pro  → Writes the code             │
│  Phase 3: REVIEW          Claude Opus → Security &amp; code review      │
│  Phase 4: FIX (if needed) Gemini Pro  → Fixes issues found          │
│  Phase 5: SUMMARY         Gemini Pro  → Executive summary for user  │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>How to use:</strong></p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>Enable Turbo Mode: <code>/turbo</code> → Select "Enable"</li>
<li>Send your request as normal text</li>
<li>The bot will automatically switch models and run all phases</li>
<li>You'll receive real-time phase updates and a final summary</li>
</ol>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">💡 Turbo Mode requires access to both Claude and Gemini models in your Antigravity subscription.</p>
</blockquote>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🎯 Goal Mode vs 🚀 Turbo Mode</h2><a id="user-content--goal-mode-vs--turbo-mode" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🎯 Goal Mode vs 🚀 Turbo Mode" href="#-goal-mode-vs--turbo-mode"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th></th>
<th>Goal Mode (<code>/goal</code>)</th>
<th>Turbo Mode (<code>/turbo</code>)</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><strong>How it works</strong></td>
<td>Agent works autonomously in a single session until done</td>
<td>Bot orchestrates multi-model pipeline externally</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>Models used</strong></td>
<td>Whichever model is currently selected</td>
<td>Claude (plan/review) + Gemini (code/fix) — automatic switching</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>Key advantage</strong></td>
<td>Simple, reliable, native IDE feature</td>
<td>Multi-model collaboration: different models cross-check each other</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>Token usage</strong></td>
<td>Single context window (efficient)</td>
<td>Multiple round-trips (more tokens)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>Progress</strong></td>
<td>🤔 reaction → final result</td>
<td>Real-time pinned message with phase updates</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>Best for</strong></td>
<td>Long tasks with a single model</td>
<td>Complex tasks benefiting from multi-model review</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>Architecture</strong></td>
<td>IDE-native (<code>/goal</code> slash command)</td>
<td>External orchestration via CDP + <code>turbo_orchestrator.js</code></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto"><strong>When to use which:</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Simple long task</strong> (e.g., "refactor this module") → <code>/goal</code></li>
<li><strong>Complex task needing cross-model review</strong> (e.g., "build this feature, review security, fix issues") → <code>/turbo</code></li>
<li><strong>Planning</strong> → <code>/plan</code> (generates plan, then you decide)</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🏗️ Architecture</h2><a id="user-content-️-architecture" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🏗️ Architecture" href="#️-architecture"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">How It Works</h3><a id="user-content-how-it-works" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: How It Works" href="#how-it-works"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="┌──────────┐     Telegram API     ┌──────────────┐     CDP (WebSocket)     ┌─────────────────┐
│ Telegram │ ◄──────────────────► │ Antigravity  │ ◄────────────────────► │ Antigravity IDE  │
│   App    │     Bot Commands     │     Bot      │    DOM Interaction     │       or         │
└──────────┘                      └──────────────┘                        │ Standalone Agent │
                                                                          └─────────────────┘"><pre class="notranslate"><code>┌──────────┐     Telegram API     ┌──────────────┐     CDP (WebSocket)     ┌─────────────────┐
│ Telegram │ ◄──────────────────► │ Antigravity  │ ◄────────────────────► │ Antigravity IDE  │
│   App    │     Bot Commands     │     Bot      │    DOM Interaction     │       or         │
└──────────┘                      └──────────────┘                        │ Standalone Agent │
                                                                          └─────────────────┘
</code></pre></div>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>You send a message via Telegram</li>
<li>The bot injects your text into the AI agent's chat input via CDP</li>
<li>The bot monitors the agent for completion (typing indicator shown in Telegram)</li>
<li>Once done, the response is extracted and sent back to Telegram</li>
<li><strong>Auto-Accept</strong>: When enabled, a MutationObserver watches for action buttons (Run, Accept, Allow, Continue) and clicks them automatically</li>
</ol>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Dual App Architecture</h3><a id="user-content-dual-app-architecture" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Dual App Architecture" href="#dual-app-architecture"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The bot supports <strong>two Antigravity applications</strong> running simultaneously:</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>App</th>
<th>Default Port</th>
<th>Config Key</th>
<th>Description</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><strong>Standalone Agent</strong></td>
<td><code>9333</code></td>
<td><code>AGENT_CDP_PORT</code></td>
<td>Lightweight chat-focused Antigravity app</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>Antigravity IDE</strong></td>
<td><code>9334</code></td>
<td><code>IDE_CDP_PORT</code></td>
<td>Full IDE with editor, terminal, and extensions</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto">Use <code>/app</code> to switch the bot's focus between apps. The <code>ANTIGRAVITY_PREFERRED_APP</code> setting in <code>.env</code> determines which app the bot targets by default.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🌐 Adding a Language</h2><a id="user-content--adding-a-language" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🌐 Adding a Language" href="#-adding-a-language"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>Copy <code>locales/en.json</code> to <code>locales/xx.json</code></li>
<li>Translate all string values</li>
<li>Set <code>LANGUAGE=xx</code> in your <code>.env</code></li>
</ol>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto"><g-emoji class="g-emoji" alias="warning">⚠️</g-emoji> Known Issues</h2><a id="user-content-️-known-issues" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: ⚠️ Known Issues" href="#️-known-issues"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Issue</th>
<th>Details</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><strong>Standalone App Limitations</strong></td>
<td>Some features (workspace switching, thread management) may not work reliably with the Standalone Antigravity App. <strong>Antigravity IDE is fully supported and recommended.</strong></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>Auto-Update on IDE 2.0</strong></td>
<td>If Antigravity IDE auto-updates, DOM selectors may break until the bot is also updated.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>Turbo Mode Model Access</strong></td>
<td>Turbo Mode requires both Claude and Gemini models to be available. If one model is unavailable, the pipeline will fail.</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">💡 As a developer, I prefer to focus on IDE support. The Standalone App integration is provided on a best-effort basis.</p>
</blockquote>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🤝 Contributing</h2><a id="user-content--contributing" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🤝 Contributing" href="#-contributing"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>Fork the repository</li>
<li>Create your feature branch (<code>git checkout -b feature/amazing-feature</code>)</li>
<li>Commit your changes (<code>git commit -m 'Add amazing feature'</code>)</li>
<li>Push to the branch (<code>git push origin feature/amazing-feature</code>)</li>
<li>Open a Pull Request</li>
</ol>
